Deux livres de chirurgie (Ambroise Paré, 1573; public domain)
===============================================================================
First edition of Paré's two books of surgery; Book II is the 'Des monstres et prodiges' — the founding text of Renaissance teratology, with its chapter on marine monsters and the siren. In French. Source: Internet Archive (https://archive.org/details/BIUSante_35181).

A  PARIS, 

Chez  André  W  echel.
